<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4D1AE0C9-6A34-43FA-8225-0C9B3B37F156'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the **smallest number** that has four different prime factors?  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First 4 prime numbers}    &= 2, 3, 5, 7 \\\\
\text{Number}                   &= 2 \times 3 \times 5 \times 7 \\\\
                                &= 210
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$210$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First 4 prime numbers}    &= 2, 3, 5, 7 \\\\
\text{Number}                   &= 2 \times 3 \times 5 \times 7 \\\\
                                &= 210
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

$92 + 29 = 47 + \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing value $=a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
92 + 29       &= 47 + a \\\\
121           &= 47 + a \\\\
a             &= 121 - 47 \\\\
a             &= 74
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$74$

</div>
</div>

a             &= 74

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$50 \times 9 = 9 \times \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing value $=a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
50 \times 9                 &= 9 \times a \\\\
9 \times a                  &= 50 \times 9 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {50 \times 9} {9} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {50 \times 9} {9} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
a                           &= 50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$50$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

a                           &= \dfrac {50 \times 9} {9} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
a                           &= 50
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$9600 \div 80 = 720 \div \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing value $=a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9600 \div 80         &= 720 \div a \\\\
\dfrac {9600} {80}   &= \dfrac {720} {a} \\\\
a                    &= \dfrac {720 \times 80} {9600} \\\\
a                    &= \dfrac {120 \times 6 \times 8 \times 10} {120 \times 8 \times 10} \\\\
a                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {120} \times 6 \times \cancel {8} \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {120} \times \cancel {8} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
a                    &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$6$

a                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {120} \times 6 \times \cancel {8} \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {120} \times \cancel {8} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
a                    &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7A3EB116-901D-4C60-9906-5C7ECB21025B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the smallest number
that you can make using the
digits $4, 3, 9$ and $1$?
Use each digit only once.

- $9413$
- $1349$
- $4319$
- $1439$
- $1934$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$1349$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$1349$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1349$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>B</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1349$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CC62F717-09D2-42A3-9042-C2C7C102D1E6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Here are three numbers
and two operations on five cards.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


Which of these gives the answer $15$?

- $24 \div 7 + 3$
- $3 + 7 \div 24$
- $7 + 3 \div 24$
- $3 \div 7 + 24$
- $24 \div 3 + 7$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using trial and error.

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 24 \div 3 + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {24} {3} + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 8} {3} + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 8} {\cancel {3}} + 7 \\\\
&= 8 + 7 \\\\
&= 15
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$24 \div 3 + 7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using trial and error.

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 24 \div 3 + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {24} {3} + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 8} {3} + 7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 8} {\cancel {3}} + 7 \\\\
&= 8 + 7 \\\\
&= 15
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$24 \div 3 + 7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 1  \dfrac {3} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {103} {100} \\\\
&= 1.03
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.03$

</div>
</div>

$
</code></pre>

\end{array}
$

<div class='question question'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 3284 \ \text {mm} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3284} {1000} \ \text {meters} \\\\
&= 3.284 \ \text {meters}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3.284\ \text{meters}$ 

</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-395EE199-8B60-4431-99FE-3444A40B71F6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many thousands are there in one million?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Count of thousands}   &= \dfrac {1000000} {1000} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1000 \times 1000} {1000} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1000 \times \cancel{1000}} {\cancel{1000}} \\\\
                            &= 1000
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1000$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Count of thousands}   &= \dfrac {1000000} {1000} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1000 \times 1000} {1000} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1000 \times \cancel{1000}} {\cancel{1000}} \\\\
                            &= 1000
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1000$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-83337D0D-9540-4245-B775-A8E101DB982C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How much is three-sevenths of $196$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text {of} \ 196 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times {196} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 196} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 28 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 28 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 28 \\\\
&= 84
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$84$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text {of} \ 196 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times {196} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 196} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 28 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 28 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 28 \\\\
&= 84
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
